{"forms":[{"form":"connotations","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The word \"advisedly\" has a connotation of \"wisely\", although it denotes merely \"intentionally\" and \"deliberately.\"","type":"example"},{"text":"The word \"happy\" has a positive connotation, while \"sad\" has a negative connotation.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A meaning of a word or phrase that is (subjectively) suggested or implied (implicit), as contrasted with a denotation, which is the explicit and objective meaning. A characteristic of words or phrases, or of the contexts that words and phrases are used in."],"synonyms":[{"word":"intension"},{"word":"intent"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","sciences","semantics"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The two expressions \"the morning star\" and \"the evening star\" have different connotations but the same denotation (i.e. the planet Venus).","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The attribute or aggregate of attributes connoted by a term, as contrasted with a denotation."],"synonyms":[{"word":"intension"},{"word":"intent"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["human-sciences","logic","mathematics","philosophy","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˌkɒnəˈteɪʃən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˌkɑnəˈteɪʃən/","tags":["General-American","dialectal"]},{"ipa":"/ˌkɒnəˈteɪʃən/","tags":["Canada","dialectal"]},{"ipa":"/ˌkɔnəˈtæɪʃən/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/ˌkɒnəˈtæɪʃən/","tags":["New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":714,"upstream_offset":5288107,"upstream_record_sha256":"8cf01c3ec60ca65f35851e63fc4e7b0d512d8d5317ce7a1e4c519401838cdede","word":"connotation"}
